inputs = jax.tree.map(lambda x: torch.from_numpy(np.array(x)).to(self._pytorch_device)[None, ...], inputs)
```

This converts numpy arrays to tensors and adds a batch dimension. If the image is `(224, 224, 3)` numpy array, it becomes `(1, 224, 224, 3)` tensor — HWC with batch dim. Then in `_preprocess_observation`:
```python
is_channels_first = image.shape[1] == 3  # Check if channels are in dimension 1


```

Since `shape[1] = 224` (not 3), `is_channels_first = False`, and the image stays HWC. Then it goes to `embed_image` which passes it to SigLIP's `get_image_features` which expects BCHW — that's the bug right there. The original `collect_activations.py` worked with pi0_base, so either the original loading path handled this differently or `obs_to_model_input` returns images in a different format already. Let me trace what `obs_to_model_input` actually returns.

So `_resize_with_pad` outputs `(224, 224, 3)` uint8 numpy arrays, and that's what gets passed through. The image then flows through LiberoInputs which restructures it, then ResizeImages which is a no-op since it's already 224x224, before hitting the policy.

```

python
def _resize_with_pad(img: np.ndarray, size: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Resize image with aspect-preserving padding to (size, size, 3) uint8."""
```

During inference, the `.infer` call adds a batch dimension making it `(1, 224, 224, 3)`, then `preprocess_observation_pytorch` doesn't permute because `is_channels_first = False`, so it stays in HWC format. But SigLIP expects CHW format `(1, 3, 224, 224)`, which means there's a shape mismatch causing a crash.

I need to check if `_parse_image` in LiberoInputs is doing the channel conversion to CHW.

argparse\nimport collections\nimport dataclasses\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport random\nimport sys\nimport types\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch._dynamo\nimport safetensors.torch\n\nfrom configs import (\n    CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING,\n    PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS,\n)\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\ntorch._dynamo.config.suppress_errors = True\n\n\ndef _ensure_openpi_imports():\n    for mod in [\n        \"lerobot\", \"lerobot.common\", \"lerobot.common.datasets\",\n        \"lerobot.common.datasets.lerobot_dataset\",\n    ]:\n        if mod not in sys.modules:\n            sys.modules[mod] = types.ModuleType(mod)\n\n\ndef _patch_preprocessing():\n    \"\"\"Patch preprocessing to ensure images are always output in CHW format.\"\"\"\n    from openpi.models_pytorch import preprocessing_pytorch as pp\n    _orig = pp.preprocess_observation_pytorch\n\n    def patched(observation, *, train=False, image_keys=pp.IMAGE_KEYS, image_resolution=pp.IMAGE_RESOLUTION):\n        result = _orig(observation, train=train, image_keys=image_keys, image_resolution=image_resolution)\n        # Force CHW format for all images\n        new_images = {}\n        for key, image in result.images.items():\n            if image.ndim == 4 and image.shape[-1] == 3:  # BHWC\n                image = image.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()\n            new_images[key] = image\n        result.images = new_images\n        return result\n\n    pp.preprocess_observation_pytorch = patched\n\n\ndef load_pi0_libero(checkpoint_path: str, device: str):\n    \"\"\"Load pi0_libero with model.* prefix stripping, no torch.compile.\"\"\"\n    _ensure_openpi_imports()\n\n    from openpi.training import config as _config\n    from openpi.models_pytorch import pi0_pytorch\n    import openpi.policies.policy as _policy\n    import openpi.transforms as transforms\n\n    _patch_preprocessing()\n\n    train_config = _config.get_config(\"pi0_libero\")\n    model_config = dataclasses.replace(train_config.model, pytorch_compile_mode=None)\n\n    model = pi0_pytorch.PI0Pytorch(config=model_config)\n\n    weight_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"model.safetensors\")\n    tensors = safetensors.torch.load_file(weight_path)\n\n    skip_prefixes = ('normalize_inputs.', 'normalize_targets.', 'unnormalize_outputs.')\n    cleaned = {}\n    for k, v in tensors.items():\n        if any(k.startswith(p) for p in skip_prefixes):\n            continue\n        cleaned[k.removeprefix('model.')] = v\n\n    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(cleaned, strict=False)\n    if missing:\n        log.warning(\"Missing keys: %d (%s)\", len(missing), missing[:3])\n\n    model.paligemma_with_expert.to_bfloat16_for_selected_params(\"bfloat16\")\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    data_config = train_config.data.create(train_config.assets_dirs, train_config.model)\n\n    policy = _policy.Policy(\n        model,\n        transforms=[\n            *data_config.data_transforms.inputs,\n            transforms.Normalize(None, use_quantiles=False),\n            *data_config.model_transforms.inputs,\n        ],\n        output_transforms=[\n            *data_config.model_transforms.outputs,\n            transforms.Unnormalize(None, use_quantiles=False),\n            *data_config.data_transforms.outputs,\n        ],\n        is_pytorch=True,\n        pytorch_device=device,\n    )\n\n    log.info(\"Loaded pi0_libero on %s (missing=%d keys)\", device, len(missing))\n    return policy, model\n\n\nfrom libero_integration import (\n    create_libero_env,\n    obs_to_model_input,\n    LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\n    MAX_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n)\nfrom collect_activations import ActivationCollector\n\n\ndef run_episode_per_step(\n    policy, collector, perturbation_type, device,\n    task_suite=\"libero_spatial\", seed=42,\n    replan_steps=DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    num_steps_wait=DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n):\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx = create_libero_env(\n        task_suite_name=task_suite,\n        perturbation_type=perturbation_type,\n        seed=seed,\n    )\n    max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite, 400)\n    step = 0\n    success = False\n\n    try:\n        trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n\n        for _ in range(num_steps_wait):\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n        action_plan = collections.deque()\n        step_activations = []\n        done = False\n\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\n                collector.clear()\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                action_chunk = result[\"actions\"]\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:replan_steps])\n\n                pooled = collector.get_pooled()\n                if pooled:\n                    step_activations.append(pooled)\n\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\n            if isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\n                action = action.cpu().numpy()\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(\n                action.tolist() if hasattr(action, \"tolist\") else list(action)\n            )\n            step += 1\n            if done or reward > 0:\n                success = info.get(\"success\", reward > 0)\n    finally:\n        env.close()\n\n    return {\n        \"step_activations\": step_activations,\n        \"num_replan_points\": len(step_activations),\n        \"task_name\": f\"{task_suite}/task_{task_idx}\",\n        \"task_description\": task_description,\n        \"num_steps\": step,\n        \"success\": success,\n    }\n\n\ndef make_labels(perturbation_type):\n    if perturbation_type == \"nominal\":\n        return {\"label_6class\": -1, \"label_3class\": -1, \"label_binary\": 0}\n    label_6 = PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS[perturbation_type]\n    label_3 = CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING[label_6]\n    return {\"label_6class\": label_6, \"label_3class\": label_3, \"label_binary\": 1}\n\n\ndef collect(args):\n    random.seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading pi0_libero from %s on %s\", args.checkpoint, args.device)\n    policy, model = load_pi0_libero(args.checkpoint, args.device)\n\n    collector = ActivationCollector(model)\n    collector.register_hooks()\n\n    if args.perturbation_type == \"all\":\n        perturbation_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys())\n    else:\n        perturbation_types = [args.perturbation_type]\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n        is_nominal = ptype == \"nominal\"\n        log.info(\"Collecting %d episodes for %s\", args.num_episodes, ptype)\n        episodes = []\n\n        for ep_idx in range(args.num_episodes):\n            log.info(\"  Episode %d/%d\", ep_idx + 1, args.num_episodes)\n            try:\n                result = run_episode_per_step(\n                    policy, collector,\n                    perturbation_type=None if is_nominal else ptype,\n                    device=args.device,\n                    task_suite=args.task_suite,\n                    seed=args.seed + ep_idx,\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                log.exception(\"Episode %d failed, skipping\", ep_idx)\n                continue\n\n            labels = make_labels(ptype)\n            episode_data = {\n                \"step_activations\": result[\"step_activations\"],\n                \"num_replan_points\": result[\"num_replan_points\"],\n                **labels,\n                \"metadata\": {\n                    \"perturbation_type\": ptype,\n                    \"task_name\": result[\"task_name\"],\n                    \"num_steps\": result[\"num_steps\"],\n                    \"success\": result[\"success\"],\n                    \"episode_idx\": ep_idx,\n                },\n            }\n            episodes.append(episode_data)\n\n        save_path = output_dir / f\"{ptype}.pt\"\n        torch.save(episodes, save_path)\n        total_steps = sum(ep[\"num_replan_points\"] for ep in episodes)\n        log.info(\"Saved %s: %d episodes, %d total steps\", save_path, len(episodes), total_steps)\n\n    collector.remove_hooks()\n    log.info(\"Collection complete.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    logging.basicConfig(\n        level=logging.INFO,\n        format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    )\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step\")\n    valid_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys()) + [\"all\", \"nominal\"]\n    p.add_argument(\"--perturbation_type\", type=str, default=\"all\", choices=valid_types)\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=25)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_spatial\",\n                   choices=[\"libero_spatial\", \"libero_object\", \"libero_goal\", \"libero_10\", \"libero_90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", type=str, default=\"cuda:1\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n    collect(args)\nSCRIPT",

{
  "server": "westd-34142",
  "command": "cat > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router_v2.sh << 'EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\n# Train PAR Router v2 on pi0_libero activations + evaluate old router on new data\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n\ncd /root/meta_brain_lora_routing\n\necho \"=== Step 3: Train new Router on v2 activations ===\"\npython3 phase1/train_par_router.py \\\n    --data_dir /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step \\\n    --layer paligemma_layer_6 \\\n    --pooling first \\\n    --gpu 1 \\\n    --epochs 50 \\\n    --lr 1e-3 \\\n    --checkpoint_dir /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/checkpoints/phase1/par_router_v2 \\\n    --output_dir /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/outputs_v2\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Step 4: Evaluate old Router (v1) on new (v2) activations ===\"\npython3 -c \"\nimport torch\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sklearn.metrics import f1_score, classification_report\nfrom collections import Counter\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, 'phase1')\nfrom train_par_router import PARRouter, load_per_step_data\n\n# Load v2 data\nX, y = load_per_step_data(\n    Path('/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step'),\n    layer='paligemma_layer_6', pooling='first'\n)\nprint(f'v2 Dataset: X={X.shape}, y={y.shape}')\nprint(f'Class distribution: {dict(Counter(y.tolist()))}')\n\n# Load old router (v1)\nckpt = torch.load('/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/checkpoints/phase1/par_router/best_router.pt',\n                  map_location='cpu', weights_only=False)\nmodel = PARRouter(\n    input_dim=ckpt['config']['input_dim'],\n    hidden_dim=ckpt['config']['hidden_dim'],\n    num_classes=ckpt['config']['num_classes'],\n    dropout=ckpt['config']['dropout'],\n)\nmodel.load_state_dict(ckpt['model_state_dict'])\nmodel.eval()\n\n# Predict\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    logits = model(X)\n    preds = logits.argmax(dim=1).numpy()\n\ny_np = y.numpy()\nf1 = f1_score(y_np, preds, average='macro')\nprint(f'\\\\nOld Router (v1) on new activations (v2): macro-F1 = {f1:.4f}')\nprint(f'Old Router (v1) on old activations (v1): macro-F1 = {ckpt[\\\"metrics\\\"][\\\"mean_f1\\\"]:.4f}')\nprint(f'Degradation: {ckpt[\\\"metrics\\\"][\\\"mean_f1\\\"] - f1:.4f}')\nprint()\nprint(classification_report(y_np, preds, target_names=['visual', 'spatial', 'physical'], digits=4))\n\"\nEOF\nchmod +x /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router_v2.sh",

## Round 1

### User

_source line 512 · 2026-05-16T12:24:21.425Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested Phase B.5: collect PaliGemma layer 6 activations from pi0_libero (fine-tuned model) and retrain the PAR Router. The original Router was trained on pi0_base activations (5-fold F1=0.9963). Since Phase C confirmed pi0_libero modified all 777 PaliGemma weights, the Router must be retrained on the new activation distribution. Steps: (1) find existing code, (2) collect pi0_libero activations into `activations_v2/per_step/`, (3) train new Router v2, (4) compare old vs new Router performance.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - PI0 model architecture: PaliGemma (vision-language) + Gemma Expert (action)
   - LeRobot vs OpenPI checkpoint formats (model.* prefix in keys)
   - Forward hooks for activation extraction on transformer layers
   - Per-step (per-replan-point) activation collection during LIBERO rollouts
   - PaliGemma layer 6 with first-token pooling → 2048-dim features
   - PAR Router: 2-layer MLP (2048→256→3) for 3-class classification (visual/spatial/physical)
   - 5-fold cross-validation with macro-F1 metric
   - torch.compile incompatibility requiring disable via `pytorch_compile_mode=None`
   - Image format issue: HWC vs CHW in preprocessing_pytorch.py

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_v2.py`
     - The main working script for collecting per-step activations from pi0_libero
     - Handles LeRobot-format checkpoint loading with `model.` prefix stripping
     - Disables torch.compile, patches preprocessing for HWC→CHW conversion
     - Key function `load_pi0_libero()` creates model, strips keys, builds Policy
     - Key function `_patch_preprocessing()` monkey-patches preprocessing to force CHW output
     - Full working code deployed on server

   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router.py`
     - Existing router training script
     - Loads per-step data via `load_per_step_data(data_dir, layer, pooling)`
     - PARRouter class: Linear(2048→256) + ReLU + Dropout(0.1) + Linear(256→3)
     - 5-fold StratifiedKFold cross-validation
     - Args: --data_dir, --layer, --pooling, --gpu, --checkpoint_dir, --output_dir

   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1/train_par_router_v2.sh`
     - Prepared bash script for Step 3 (train v2 router) and Step 4 (evaluate old router on new data)
     - Trains with: --data_dir activations_v2/per_step --layer paligemma_layer_6 --pooling first --gpu 1
     - Evaluates old router (v1) on v2 activations and reports degradation

   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/libero_integration.py`
     - `load_pi0_for_collection(checkpoint_path, device, config_name="pi0_libero")` - original loader
     - `run_episode()` - runs LIBERO rollout, returns averaged activations
     - `create_libero_env()` - creates perturbation-specific LIBERO environments
     - `obs_to_model_input()` - converts LIBERO observations to policy input format

   - `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/configs.py`
     - PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS: camera_viewpoints=0, light_conditions=1, background_textures=2, sensor_noise=3, objects_layout=4, robot_initial_states=5
     - CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING: {0-3: visual(0), 4: spatial(1), 5: physical(2)}
     - PALIGEMMA_LAYERS = [2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16]
     - PALIGEMMA_LAYER_TEMPLATE = "paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.language_model.layers.{i}"

   - `/root/openpi/src/openpi/models_pytorch/preprocessing_pytorch.py`
     - Has a bug: HWC inputs stay HWC when `is_channels_first=False`, never converts to CHW for model
     - Fixed via monkey-patch in collect_activations_v2.py

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero_openpi/model.safetensors`
     - Converted checkpoint with stripped `model.` prefix (777 keys)
     - Not actually used since collect_activations_v2.py does stripping in-memory

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Keys mismatch (model.* prefix)**: pi0_libero checkpoint saved in LeRobot format with `model.` prefix + normalize buffers. Fixed by stripping `model.` prefix and skipping normalize_inputs/normalize_targets/unnormalize_outputs keys during load_state_dict.
   - **Wrong module path**: `openpi.models.pi0` doesn't have `PI0Pytorch`. Fixed: correct import is `from openpi.models_pytorch import pi0_pytorch` → class `pi0_pytorch.PI0Pytorch`.
   - **Wrong transforms import**: `from openpi.shared import transforms` doesn't exist. Fixed: `import openpi.transforms as transforms`.
   - **torch.compile failure**: `pytorch_compile_mode='max-autotune'` in pi0_libero config causes dynamo errors during inference. Fixed: `dataclasses.replace(train_config.model, pytorch_compile_mode=None)` + `torch._dynamo.config.suppress_errors = True`.
   - **Image HWC→CHW**: Images arrive as (B,224,224,3) but SigLIP expects (B,3,224,224). `preprocessing_pytorch.py` detects `is_channels_first=False` but never converts back to CHW. Fixed: monkey-patch `preprocess_observation_pytorch` to permute all HWC images to CHW after processing.
   - **Missing key**: `paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.embed_tokens.weight` is missing (1 key) — this is a tied/shared weight, not critical.
   - **Norm stats not found**: Warned but gracefully skipped. Not critical for activation collection since PaliGemma layer 6 activations are primarily driven by visual tokens.

Looking at the timing:
- 20:14:50 - Started camera_viewpoints
- 20:21:54 - Finished camera (7 min for 25 episodes)
- 20:21:54 - Started light_conditions


- 20:26:43 - Episode 15/25 of light_conditions (~5 min for 15 episodes)
- 20:27:25 - Currently episode 16 or 17 (running)

At roughly 20-25 seconds per episode, I'm estimating light_conditions will finish in about 3-4 minutes, then background_textures, sensor_noise, objects_layout, and robot_initial_states each taking 8-10 minutes. That puts the total remaining time at around 35-40 minutes from now.

Since I can't rely on ScheduleWakeup outside of a loop context, I'll check back periodically with less verbose updates.

Actually, the "Episode X/25" log prints at the START of each episode, not the end. So those 50 matches represent 25 started episodes for light_conditions plus 25 for camera_viewpoints. The 25th episode of light_conditions just started and is currently running, so once it finishes the save will appear and background_textures will begin collecting.
